* fix(cache): tolerate small future cache timestamps

Windows file mtimes can be slightly ahead of time(), which made Cache.new discard a just-written cache and repeat successful DNS writes.

Co-authored-by: Copilot App <223556219+Copilot@users.noreply.github.com>

* fix(docker): wait for startup processes on SIGTERM

The entrypoint shell exited as soon as tini forwarded SIGTERM, so container teardown could kill the initial DDNS child before it handled the signal.

80813a07144ed1514a8448330ecdc2459dcfcb051600e57aff994e751c31305bcebf0580348ce248Copilot198982749+Copilot@users.noreply.github.com2025-10-15T14:00:14Zfix(config): restore log.file configuration for v4.1 multi-provider format (#564)

* Initial plan

* fix(config): restore log.file configuration for v4.1 multi-provider format

The _setup_logging function was incorrectly using an empty dict for log settings
when there were multiple providers in v4.1 format. This caused log.file and other
log configuration to be ignored.

Changed the condition from:
  json_config = all_json_configs[0] if len(all_json_configs) == 1 else {}
To:
  json_config = all_json_configs[0] if all_json_configs else {}

Since all provider configs inherit the same global log settings in v4.1 format,
we can safely use the first config for logging setup regardless of the number
of providers.

Move sys and logging imports from inside test method to the top of the file,
following standard Python import conventions and matching the pattern used
in other test files in the repository.

Renamed PrintProvider to DebugProvider and updated all references in the codebase, including configuration defaults and schema. This change standardizes the provider naming and improves clarity by using 'debug' instead of 'print' for the default DNS provider.

77818615c041b00aa8aeecba1bb5b551b15b170f732c5f8c0e5dfd77676d94e47cd55c1653a939cdElec4d056445378+Elec4d0@users.noreply.github.com2024-06-27T10:58:08+08:00希望能更改linux系统下，index6 正则获取本地ip的命令优先级 (#441)

更改linux系统下，index6 正则获取本地ip的命令优先级

优先使用ip address 而非ifconfig，理由如下：
ifconfig 会先匹配到处于弃用（Deprecated）状态的v6 ip，不会把处于弃用的v6放到末尾，导致提交的dns v6解析记录为过期v6 ip。

而ip address获取到的前两个ip均为有效前缀v6 ip，可能是对lifetime 或者 ip 获得时间进行过排序

ipaddr 与 win 系统下的ipconfig ip顺序一致，符合“排序”期望

故希望在linux系统中，优先考虑使用ipaddr 而非 ipconfig
